2026-10-14T16:56:08.1777500Z act(() => {
2026-10-14T16:56:08.1778130Z   /* fire events that update state */
2026-10-14T16:56:08.1778840Z });
2026-10-14T16:56:08.1779370Z /* assert on the output */
2026-10-14T16:56:08.1779800Z 
2026-10-14T16:56:08.1781570Z This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act
